## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 작업을 대신 맡아 처리할 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 편하게 쉬어요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPとUDPの一番の違いは、**TCPは届いたことを保証する「信頼性のある」通信、UDPは保証しない代わりに速くて軽い通信**という点です。

TCPは届いたか確認し、欠けたものは再送し、順番も揃えて渡します。UDPはそれをせず、送りっぱなしにします。Webやメールはデータの欠落が困るのでTCP、動画配信やオンラインゲームのように遅延を避けたい場合はUDPがよく使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Rebase reescribe tu rama sobre la base para dejar el historial lineal; úsalo en ramas locales o privadas que nadie más ha usado. Merge une las ramas conservando el historial real, y es lo seguro para ramas compartidas o ya publicadas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的：保证数据完整、按序送达，代价是更慢、开销更大；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但快、开销小，适合直播、游戏、DNS 这类更看重速度的场景。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for your own branch that hasn't been shared yet, like tidying up local commits before opening a PR. Merge when the branch is shared or you want to keep the true history, since rebasing rewrites commits and can cause problems for anyone who already pulled them.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，登录页分三步做：先换输入框组件，再接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，一件做完再开下一件，做完告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换成新的输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话：“~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。” ⏎  ⏎ 这是第一件：把登录表单里的输入框换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎ - 登录页的表单输入框全部改用新的输入框组件，行为和原来一致（输入、校验提示、禁用态）。 ⏎ - 完成标准：登录页能正常渲染，相关的现有测试和类型检查通过；报告里附上跑过的命令和结果。 ⏎ - 范围外：不接短信验证码（那是下一件）；不补端到端测试（再下一件）；不合并、不推送、不开 PR。在自己的分支上提交即可。 ⏎ - 地点：仓库 ~/dev/ducter。
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 登录页：换成新的输入框组件 (working); 2. 登录页：接上短信验证码 (working); 3. 登录页：补端到端测试 (working)
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接在 `bun test` 后面跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果想只跑某个用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例名"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it's applied to the actual data, so that if the system crashes midway, it can replay the log to recover without losing committed work. It trades a little extra write work up front for durability and fast, safe recovery.
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<script>
(function () {
  function fmt(n) { return n == null ? '—' : '¥' + n; }
  function render() {
    var rows = ducter.get('trains') || [];
    var cheap = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.price < 600; });
    var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
    var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    cnt.textContent = cheap.length + ' 班';
    var low = cheap.length ? Math.min.apply(null, cheap.map(function (r) { return r.price; })) : null;
    var tight = cheap.filter(function (r) { return r.seats != null && r.seats <= 5; });
    sub.textContent = low == null ? '暂时没有 600 以下的班次'
      : '最低 ' + fmt(low) + (tight.length ? ' · ' + tight.map(function (r) { return r.no + ' 只剩 ' + r.seats + ' 张'; }).join('，') : '');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = r.no + ' 二等座';
      var s = document.createElement('div');
      s.className = 'k-row-sub';
      s.textContent = (r.seats == null ? '余票未知' : '余 ' + r.seats + ' 张') + (r.price < 600 ? ' · 600 以下' : '');
      main.appendChild(s);
      var v = document.createElement('div');
      v.className = 'k-row-value';
      v.style.color = r.price < 600 ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
      v.textContent = fmt(r.price);
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(v);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    if (!rows.length) {
      var e = document.createElement('div');
      e.className = 'k-empty';
      e.textContent = '还没有票价数据，每天早上会自动更新。';
      list.appendChild(e);
    }
  }
  render();
  ducter.onChange(render);
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}

</style>
<main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">No runs yet</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latestSub"></div>
  </section>

  <div class="k-card">

<script>
function render(){
  var nights = ducter.get('nights') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  document.getElementById('empty').hidden = nights.length > 0;
  if (!nights.length) {
    document.getElementById('latest').textContent = 'No runs yet';
    document.getElementById('latestSub').textContent = '';
    return;
  }
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('latestSub').textContent = last.name + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function(n){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = n.name;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · stalls ' + n.stalls;
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = n.writes + '/s';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"name":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"name":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

  const [h, m] = t.split(':');
  feeds.push({ t: dayKey() + ' ' + pad(h) + ':' + m, ml });

<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><p class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有花销，下面记第一笔。</p></div>
<h2 class="k-h" id="formTitle">记一笔</h2>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips"><button class="k-chip" id="c1" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" id="c2" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
<button class="k-btn" id="reset">清空</button>
<button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这一笔</button>
</div>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通", editing=null;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const items=()=>ducter.get("items")||[];
const fmt=n=>"¥"+Number(n|| … lassName="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.trip||"出差";
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=(x.day||"")+" "+(x.cat||"");
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(x.amt);
    r.append(m,s,v);r.onclick=()=>edit(i);list.append(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total));
}
function edit(i){
  const x=items()[i];editing=i;
  $("trip").value=x.trip||"";$("day").value=x.day||"";$("amt").value=x.amt||"";setCat(x.cat||"交通");
  $("formTitle").textContent="改这一笔";$("del").hidden=false;
}
$("c1").onclick=()=>setCat("交通");
$("c2").onclick=()=>setCat("住宿");
$("reset").onclick=reset;
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=$("amt").value.trim();
  if(!amt)return;
  const obj={trip:$("trip").value.trim(),day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:Number(amt),from:(editing!==null?items()[editing].from:undefined)};
  const xs=items();
  if(editing!==null)xs[editing]=obj;else xs.push(obj);
  ducter.set("items",xs);reset();draw();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{
  if(editing===null)return;
  const xs=items();xs.splice(editing,1);
  ducter.set("items",xs);reset();draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
reset();draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"from":"你的预订"}]}

```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个 3D 风格的插画：一台米白色的笔记本电脑，深藏青色的屏幕边框，屏幕是空白的，键盘和触控板也画得很清楚，背景是透明的。

很适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好对应"这里还没有内容"的意思，米白色也柔和，不会让人觉得冷清或出错。320×246 的尺寸，显示在 160 到 200 像素宽的位置上正合适，细节还看得清。

有两点要注意。深色模式下，米白色的主体在深色背景上会很显眼，可能要单独调一下亮度。另外，图本身不带文字，旁边要配一句说明和一个明确的下一步按钮，比如"还没有记录，点这里新建一条"。
```
